Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The mcmc variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 831 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 258 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by mcmc generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 831, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 258. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The mcmc operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 613. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 52 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on mcmc structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 613. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 52. QED.
